# P4 automatic-change review ledger

- **Change:** `adopt-ruff-green-checks`
- **Phase:** P4 (safe automatic fixes) only. P5–P7 are not started.
- **Input state:** [`p4-source-before.sha256`](p4-source-before.sha256), identical to the accepted P3 formatted state.
- **Accepted output state:** [`p4-source-after.sha256`](p4-source-after.sha256). This manifest was rechecked with `sha256sum -c` (exit 0).
- **Diagnostics before:** [`p4-ruff-findings-before.txt`](p4-ruff-findings-before.txt) — **1006 findings, exit 1**.
- **Residual diagnostics:** [`p4-ruff-findings-residual.txt`](p4-ruff-findings-residual.txt) — **667 findings, exit 1**, handed to P5 unresolved.

## Work-order completeness

This ledger is block-level, not file-level. It contains **317 accepted work orders**:

| Class | Work orders | Accounting |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Changed import blocks | 311 (`P4-R001`–`P4-R311`) | 190 source files; 184 module-scope and 127 function/class/conditional-scope blocks |
| Independent semantic work orders | 5 (`P4-R312`–`P4-R316`) | six `F541` literal fixes: two separate runtime diagnostics, two adjacent host-access test literals (one work order), one phase-4 test literal, one npm test literal |
| Generated test fixture | 1 (`P4-R317`) | `tests/fixtures/process-architecture-inventory.json` |
| **Total** | **317** | **191 changed source files + one generated fixture** |

The 191 changed source files comprise 190 files with at least one changed import block plus `tests/test_npm_environment_policy.py`, whose only source change is the semantic `F541` fix in `P4-R316`. The previous file-level `P4-R191` (`docker/versioning/runtime_verification.py`, both diagnostics) is split into `P4-R312` (`gateway.mapping`) and `P4-R313` (`host-access.address`).

## How to read each work order

Every row has a completion checkbox and is accepted only against the checked source state. `P4-B` is the prerequisite: the target is present in [`p4-source-before.sha256`](p4-source-before.sha256), the accepted automatic fix was applied without a handwritten source repair, and the target is present in [`p4-source-after.sha256`](p4-source-after.sha256). Source fingerprints were not changed by this documentation-only ledger expansion.

Verification aliases (exact commands):

- `V1`: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests -n 8 --dist loadfile --tb=short -q` — exit 0, **7991 passed, 13 skipped, 11 warnings, 18026 subtests passed**.
- `V2`: `scripts/check-types` — exit 0, `All checks passed!`.
- `V3`: `scripts/check-python-compile` — exit 0, no diagnostics for the 396-path inventory.
- `V4`: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_diagnostic_recognizer_phase3.py tests/test_host_download_observability.py tests/test_version_effective.py tests/test_constructor_verification.py tests/enforcement/test_descriptor_close_convergence.py tests/enforcement/test_process_contract.py tests/enforcement/test_process_integrated_convergence.py tests/enforcement/test_layout_independence.py -n 8 --dist loadfile --tb=short -q` — exit 0, **385 passed, 698 subtests passed**; used for cycle-sensitive blocks.
- `V5`: `scripts/check-python-format` — exit 0, `396 files already formatted`.
- `V6`: `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_constructor_runtime_verification.py tests/test_constructor_host_access_acceptance.py tests/test_constructor_phase4_named_context.py tests/test_npm_environment_policy.py -n 8 --dist loadfile --tb=short -q` — exit 0, **69 passed, 1 skipped**; used for semantic fixes.

`P4-B` plus the named test command is the acceptance prerequisite for each row. The result column records the checked before/after relation and the passing result; residuals and environment limits are listed at the end.

## Rejected automatic changes

The mandated full-inventory safe-fix run was executed first:

```bash
.venv/bin/ruff check --no-cache --fix --output-format concise -- "${inventory[@]}"
```

It exited **1** with `1023 errors (653 fixed, 370 remaining)` and changed 215 source files. Review rejected three classes of its output:

1. **All `F401` removals were rejected.** The repository uses intentional re-export modules and test-module symbol re-exports. Removing their imports broke collection: the diagnostic full pytest run reported **263 failed, 3445 passed, 284 errors, 15633 subtests passed** with `ImportError`s such as `docker.transactions.errors.carry_secondary_diagnostics`, `tests.test_process_stop_policy.api` and the `docker.versions` shim. Restoring those files and keeping F401 as residual backlog preserves the public/import boundary; P5 owns the per-target decision to remove, re-export explicitly, or suppress with a rationale.
2. **All `RUF100` removals were also rejected.** `RUF100` must be evaluated with the complete configured rule context. A narrowed replay removed still-needed `# noqa: E402` directives in `scripts/python_checks.py`, `scripts/validate-phase5` and `tests/test_constructor_migration_contract.py`. Obsolete suppressions remain residual backlog for P5 review rather than being removed without that context.
3. **The `B010` rewrite was rejected.** `setattr(error, "cleanup", self)` → `error.cleanup = self` in `docker/process.py` made the configured type check fail: `scripts/check-types` exited **1** with `error[unresolved-attribute] Unresolved attribute cleanup on type BaseException` at both sites. The original dynamic assignment is restored; the two findings remain residual P5 work.

The rejected output is not concealed: its effects are absent from the accepted delta and its remaining findings are in the residual inventory. The accepted delta was produced only by automatic tooling after restoring the captured before-state; no handwritten source repair or new regression test was added.

Accepted command:

```bash
.venv/bin/ruff check --no-cache --fix --select I001,E401,F541,B010 --output-format concise -- "${inventory[@]}"
```

Result: **exit 0 — `Found 332 errors (332 fixed, 0 remaining)`**. The review then rejected the two `B010` sites, so the retained automatic delta contains **330 fixes across 191 source files**. Changed inventory files were formatted with `ruff format --no-cache -- "${changed[@]}"`: **exit 0 — files left unchanged**. The full-inventory format check exits 0.

AST review of the accepted 191 source files: **0 unique import symbols/modules added, 0 removed**; one duplicate identical `OverridePolicy` import was collapsed to one binding. The changed blocks are 232 `I001` reorders, 53 layout/comma-only changes (19 E401 comma splits and 34 line-layout-only blocks) and 26 whitespace-only inter-block blank-line normalizations. The source delta is 191 files (`+813`/`−741`) plus the generated test-owned inventory regeneration (`+385`/`−385`).

## Completeness and consistency checks

A local ledger check exited **0** after re-deriving the block inventory from the 396-file AST state and parsing the revised tables. It verified:

- 311 unique changed import-block keys, with no duplicate or missing block; 190 block files plus `tests/test_npm_environment_policy.py` account for all 191 changed source files.
- Exactly one accepted row for each of those changed blocks and one row for each of the five semantic work orders (six `F541` literals); `P4-R312` and `P4-R313` replace the old combined runtime diagnostic row.
- All import-block rows retain the same final normalized local-name binding map. `tests/test_version_updates.py` collapses a duplicate identical `OverridePolicy` binding from two imports to one; `docker/versioning/transports.py` reorders two `urllib` submodule imports while keeping the `urllib` package binding and both submodules loaded.
- All 317 work orders have a completion checkbox, `P4-B` prerequisite, before/after evidence, `Result: PASS`, and a `V1` or `V4`/`V6` exact-command alias; the 317 IDs are contiguous `P4-R001`–`P4-R317` with no duplicates.

## Residual backlog for P5

The complete remaining location/rule inventory is [`p4-ruff-findings-residual.txt`](p4-ruff-findings-residual.txt) (667 findings): 247 `F401`, 122 `E402`, 79 `B023`, 44 `B904`, 42 `F841`, 26 `F811`, 21 `RUF100`, 20 `B905`, 16 `B017`, 12 `F821`, 10 `E731`, 7 `E741`, 7 `B007`, 6 `B018`, 4 `SIM115`, 2 `B010`, 2 `B008`. It includes every rejected automatic change and is explicitly unresolved, never a passing lint check.

## Limitations

- The Dockerfile gate was not run because the Docker CLI/daemon is absent; `Dockerfile` is unchanged.
- Full-inventory lint remains P5 backlog at 667 findings, exit 1; no lint-green claim is made.
- The 13 full-suite skips are the reviewed environmental/opt-in skips: three need a `docker-dev` account, one needs `sudo runuser -u nobody`, eight are Phase-6 Docker-backed opt-in/standalone-runner cases, and one needs `NPM_ENV_REAL_DOCKER=1` plus Docker.
- Test results are local; no remote CI run was performed. No version-control staging, unstage, reset or commit was performed by this work.
